class="jsx-4104793640"><div class="jsx-4104793640 container"><h1 class="jsx-4104793640">About Our Data</h1><p class="jsx-4104793640">We collect actual mobile app usage data from panel users and calculate statistical estimates that are theoretically close to the real numbers. We are proud of our capability of analyzing data persistently and quantitatively.</p><div><div class="jsx-4104793640 cards not-entered"><div class="jsx-4104793640"><div class="jsx-2073808962 card"><figure class="jsx-2073808962"><img alt="Actual Usage Data Don't Lie" class="jsx-2073808962" src="/static/images/lp/our-data/our-data-3@2x.png"/></figure><h2 class="jsx-2073808962"><span class="jsx-2073808962">Actual Usage Data</span><span class="jsx-2073808962">Don't Lie</span></h2><p class="jsx-2073808962">Unlike questionnaires, you can't get results that are false or untrue from actual mobile app usage data. Our data are actual usage data directly collected from mobile users' devices and statistical estimates based on that, which allows us to provide more accurate information than the usual qualitative research methods.</p></div></div><div class="jsx-4104793640"><div class="jsx-2073808962 card"><figure class="jsx-2073808962"><img alt="Statistically Sufficient Sample Size" class="jsx-2073808962" src="/static/images/lp/our-data/our-data-2@2x.png"/></figure><h2 class="jsx-2073808962"><span class="jsx-2073808962">Statistically Sufficient</span><span class="jsx-2073808962">Sample Size</span></h2><p class="jsx-2073808962">App Ape possesses data sent from up to 700,000 users each month. Among them, we extract up to 50,000 panel’s user data from each country to calculate data which becomes various indices in App Ape. Based on 95% of the confidence interval of the statistical method, the standard error is set to fall within 0.44% to 1.4%.</p></div></div><div class="jsx-4104793640"><div class="jsx-2073808962 card"><figure class="jsx-2073808962"><img alt="Our Method for Less Biased Data" class="jsx-2073808962" src="/static/images/lp/our-data/our-data-1@2x.png"/></figure><h2 class="jsx-2073808962"><span class="jsx-2073808962">Our Method for Less</span><span class="jsx-2073808962">Biased Data</span></h2><p class="jsx-2073808962">We Strive to improve the accuracy of our estimates by using sample weighting method that aggregates collected samples according to a segmentation of the population in order to prevent the imbalance that occurs in the process of selecting data from samples and to reflect the market composition of smartphone users.</p></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="jsx-1599501066"><div class="jsx-1599501066 container"><h1 class="jsx-1599501066">How App Ape Compares</h1><p class="jsx-1599501066">You need more Usage Data? App Ape has them. See how App Ape Compares.</p><div class="jsx-1599501066 logos"><figure class="jsx-1599501066"><img alt="Apptopia" class="jsx-1599501066" Apeでの各種指標となるデータを算出しております。統計学的手法の信頼区間の95%を基準に標準誤差を0.44%から1.4%の間に収まるように定めています。*標準誤差は各国のパネル数により国ごとに異なります。","item3_title_l1":"データ集計方法の偏りを","item3_title_l2":"補正する集計方法","item3_description":"サンプルからのデータ選出の際に起こる偏りを防ぎスマホユーザーの市場構成を映し出すため、回収されたサンプルを母集団の構成にあわせて集計するウェイトバック集計を用い推計精度の向上を図っています。その結果、現在ではサンプルパネル数・調査対象アプリ数・
